## 224. Raw Reality TV Broadcast Screenshots (2x2 Grid)

- **id**：4997
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=4997

### 描述

A complex, structured prompt designed to generate a 2x2 grid of images mimicking raw, unpolished TV broadcast footage, specifically in the style of a reality show like Big Brother. It mandates on-screen graphics (timestamps, camera IDs) and specific visual aesthetics for four different scenes: Domestic Friction, Confessional Booth, Night Vision Secrets, and The Explosion.

### 示例图

![Raw Reality TV Broadcast Screenshots (2x2 Grid)](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767966010458_blfvy6_G-DrF0pW0AAFfQa.jpg)

### 提示词

```
**CORE INSTRUCTION FOR THE MODEL:**
Analyze the personality traits and potential conflicts of the CHARACTERS previously provided as input. You must infer how these specific individuals would interact and place them into the four scenarios below based on their likelihood to be involved in that specific type of scene (e.g., put gossips in the confessional, aggressors in the fight scene).

**MANDATORY VISUAL AESTHETIC ("Real Broadcast Look"):**
The images must NOT look like staged photography. They must look like raw, unpolished TV broadcast footage.
* **Crucial Details:** Every panel must include on-screen graphics: timestamps (e.g., "DAY 12 | 14:32"), camera location identifiers (e.g., "CAM 1 - LIVING AREA"), and a small, semi-transparent channel logo in a corner.
* **Textures:** Expect video grain, slight lens distortion, harsh fluorescent lighting, and occasional digital compression artifacts.

**GRID BREAKDOWN (2x2 Layout):**

**[TOP ROW]**
* **[PANEL 1: Top-Left] Domestic Friction (Kitchen/Living Area).** Wide-angle fixed CCTV camera shot. Harsh, unflattering overhead lighting. Based on inputs, show a tense moment of daily life—two characters with incompatible habits arguing over cleaning or food, while others sit uncomfortably in the background. The composition is messy and naturalistic.
* **[PANEL 2: Top-Right] The Confessional Booth (El Confesionario).** Tight, claustrophobic fish-eye lens close-up. The background is a simple, soundproofed wall. Select the character most likely to vent or gossip from the inputs. They are looking directly into the lens, highly emotional (crying or angrily gesturing), breaking the fourth wall.

**[BOTTOM ROW]**
* **[PANEL 3: Bottom-Left] Night Vision Secrets (Bedroom).** Monochrome green or grey infrared night-vision shot. Grainy, low fidelity. Total darkness in the room. Show two characters (strategic allies based on inputs) whispering conspiratorially in a bed, barely visible under covers.
* **[PANEL 4: Bottom-Right] The Explosion (Garden/Smoking Area).** Chaotic, slightly shaky "handheld camera" feel, as if an operator ran to catch the action. High drama. A screaming argument at a party or nighttime gathering. The most volatile characters from the inputs are being physically separated by others. Flashing lights or dramatic shadows.

**STYLE:** Raw reality TV broadcast screenshots, 2x2 grid, highly detailed, 4k
```

## 225. Hyper-realistic crossover image of Zenitsu Agatsuma from Demon Slayer

- **id**：4964
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=4964

### 描述

A detailed, hyper-realistic prompt for Nano Banana Pro that generates an image of Zenitsu Agatsuma from the anime 'Demon Slayer' stepping out of a giant physical manga book, blending the 8K photo-realistic world with the black-and-white manga panel style.

### 示例图

![Hyper-realistic crossover image of Zenitsu Agatsuma from Demon Slayer](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1767804115206_v8i4fj_G9_MHmObcAA8Bsg.jpg)

### 提示词

```
An astonishing hyper-realistic 8K photograph capturing the moment {argument name="character name" default="Zenitsu Agatsuma"} from "{argument name="manga name" default="Demon Slayer"}" steps out of a giant physical manga book.

Zenitsu in the real world:
Zenitsu's image is photorealistic, with messy gold and orange hair, wearing a realistically textured Demon Slayer Corps uniform and his iconic yellow haori with white triangle patterns. His expression is determined, his eyes sharp, full of resolve, tightly gripping his Nichirin blade in its sheath. Half of his body has stepped onto the wooden bedroom floor, while the other half remains within the manga pages.
The giant manga book and interaction:
A giant, thick Japanese edition of "Demon Slayer" manga book is spread open on a slightly worn wooden floor. Zenitsu breaks the boundary of the paper, and the pages are violently torn, curled, and burst where he steps out, with paper scraps and toner flying in the air.

Details within the manga page (picture-in-picture):
The pages display an extremely authentic black-and-white Japanese manga style.
Style: Rough black ink lines, rich screentone textures for shading, exaggerated speed lines.

Layout: Strict Japanese right-to-left reading order, a surreal four-panel manga (yonkoma) spread.
Content: Inside the broken panel, a trapped, manga-style Zenitsu can be seen. He is wearing the same clothes, shedding noodle tears in the panel, frantically knocking on the borders of the manga panel, accompanied by large Japanese onomatopoeia like "出られない!" (Can't get out!), "えええ!?", and "ドンドンドン" (Knocking sound). The manga version of him is watching the real-world version of himself tear the frame in terror.

Environment and lighting:
The bedroom background is soft-focused, with domestic items like bed legs and bookshelves. Natural afternoon light streams in from the side window, illuminating the edges of the torn paper and the fabric texture of Zenitsu's clothes, emphasizing the strong contrast between reality and the two-dimensional world.
```

## 249. Nano Banana Pro Manga Generation Prompt with Character Consistency Rules

- **id**：3630
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=3630

### 描述

A detailed system prompt for Nano Banana Pro aimed at generating manga pages while strictly maintaining character consistency based on an uploaded reference image. It includes specific rules for fixing character features (hair, eyes, clothing) as 'master data,' applying a chibi style only to body proportions, and enforcing strict rules for natural Japanese dialogue and camera work to avoid monotony.

### 示例图

![Nano Banana Pro Manga Generation Prompt with Character Consistency Rules](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766987765739_xcs422_G9NTiyVagAArRvR.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Please generate a manga page.

#Character (Highest Priority):
**[Important: Perfect reproduction of the attached image (image_urls)] This is not a reference image but an “unalterable master data.” Strictly adhere to the following processes in all generated panels.**

1. **Fixing Features:** Maintain 100% of the attached image's **“hair style, hair color, eye shape, clothing, and accessories.”** Unauthorized arrangements or simplifications are prohibited.
2. **Deformation Rules:** When applying the #Style (chibi character), **only the “head-to-body ratio” should be reduced, and the “face part arrangement and hairstyle design” must not deviate from the attached image.**
3. **Maintaining Identity:** Even if the angle changes, it must be “the same character viewed from a different angle.” Do not change the face to look like a different person.
4. **Do not cause “Prompt Bleed (instruction leakage).”**

#Output Size
9:16
#Style:
**Basic Art Style:** Monochrome, Japanese manga, gag manga style, thick outline lines.
**Style prompt:** Japanese manga, chibi style, thick outline lines, monochrome.
**Format:** Vertical writing, Japanese panel layout read from right to left.

#Text Generation Rules (Strict Adherence):
**[Important: Natural Japanese and Elimination of Duplication]**
When generating dialogue in the manga, perform proofreading immediately before output to absolutely prevent the following errors:

1. **Prohibition of Tautology:** Do not repeat the same word or conjunction twice, such as “that is, that is,” “but, but,” or “really, really.” Stuttering expressions are prohibited.
2. **Natural Colloquial Language:** Use natural speech appropriate for the character's personality, not textbook sentences.
3. **Garbled Text Check:** Confirm that Kanji and Hiragana are not corrupted.

#Direction/Camera Work:
**[Important: Direction while maintaining features]**
Use the following camera work within the range that does not distort the character's face or hairstyle to avoid monotony:

- **Close-up:** Emphasizing emotion (※Do not overly deform facial parts)
- **Long Shot:** Explaining the situation
- **High/Low Angle:** Expressing impact or cuteness
- **Diagonal Composition:** Expressing agitation or action

#Scenario Progression:
If no specific panel designation is given, summarize and compose in 3 to 5 panels.
Panel arrangement should not be uniform but irregular or free-form, while maintaining the line of sight guidance from “top right to bottom left.”

#Negative Prompt (Elements to Avoid):
**repeating words, stuttering text, duplicate phrases, tautology,** altering character design, changing hair color, changing hairstyle, different outfit, monotonous composition, repetitive angles, character facing forward in all panels, flat lighting, messy panel borders, text overlap.

#Scenario: {argument name="scenario" default="Think of the continuation of the attached manga."}
```

## 259. Character Inner Monologue Comic Prompt (Japanese)

- **id**：3511
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=3511

### 描述

A complex, multi-step prompt template designed for Nano Banana Pro to generate a Japanese comic panel visualizing a character's 'inner monologue' or 'brainstorming session.' It requires user input for character settings and a dilemma, then automatically assigns roles (Optimistic, Pessimistic, Logical, My-Pace) to four mini-characters (chibi-style) who appear above the main character's head, each with a specific pose and dialogue bubble.

### 示例图

![Character Inner Monologue Comic Prompt (Japanese)](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766940208307_7jqbzk_G8_CKQmb0AQf_lQ.jpg)

### 提示词

```
# User Input (Rewrite and submit this)
【Character Settings】
{argument name="character settings" default="Brown hair, twin tails, red eyes, fangs, black cloak, white shirt"}

【Main Character's Worry】
*   Content: {argument name="worry" default="I want to eat lots of donuts"}

# Command
You are an image generation engine. Follow the steps below to generate one image based on the user's input.
**Do not output intermediate thoughts or script text. Generate the image immediately.**
# Process (Internal Processing)
1. **Automatic Dialogue Determination**:
*   **Main Character**: Monologues the 【Worry】 input by the user.
*   **Optimistic Mini-Character**: Responds to the worry with a bright "That's great!" and offers support/encouragement.
*   **Pessimistic Mini-Character**: Anticipates the worst-case scenario for the worry, saying "No good..." and despairs.
*   **My-Pace Mini-Character**: **【IMPORTANT】Talks about something completely unrelated to the worry (e.g., tonight's dinner, sleepiness, hobbies).**
*   **Logical Mini-Character**: **【IMPORTANT】Performs a coin toss and randomly decides to speak either "For" (presenting a rational solution) or "Against" (pointing out risks).**

2. **Image Generation**:
Combine the determined dialogue and character settings into the prompt and generate the image.
### Image Generation Prompt Requirements
*   **Aspect Ratio**: 2:3
*   **Style**: Japanese comic/anime style, high quality.
*   **Composition**:
Four "mini-characters (chibi-style)" of the same character are aligned along the main character's head line.
【IMPORTANT】The four mini-characters are lined up "in a horizontal row."
【IMPORTANT】The feet of the mini-characters are hidden (overlapping) by the main character's head or hair.
*   **【IMPORTANT: Prohibitions】**:
*   **Absolutely do not write names or roles (e.g., "Logical," "Optimistic") below or around the characters.**
*   **The only place text is allowed is inside the "speech bubbles."**
*   Bottom 1/3 of the screen: Extreme close-up of the main character's face. Serious expression. Thinking, hand on chin or mouth, gaze downward. A large horizontal thought bubble.
*   Top 2/3 of the screen: Four mini-characters are lined up "in a horizontal row" right behind the main character's head.
*   **Important**: The mini-characters are close enough that their feet are hidden by the main character's head or hair.
*   **Mini-Character Arrangement and Pose**:
From left to right:
1. Logical: Serious face, adjusting glasses, or pointing with a finger.
2. My-Pace: Blank face, yawning, or drinking tea.
3. Pessimistic: Pale face, holding head in despair, vertical line effect.
4. Optimistic: Full smile, hands raised in joy, music note mark.
*   **Speech Bubbles**: Add comic-style speech bubbles to each character.
```

```
# Image Generation Prompt: Nano Banana Pro v1.2
# 1. Basic Instructions
- Task: Image Generation
- Reference: Faithfully reproduce and maintain the character, clothing, and image style of the attached image
- Quality: Super High Definition

# 2. Technical Specifications
- Aspect Ratio: 16:9
- Rendering Style: Based on the attached image style, apply Cell Shading + Volumetric Lighting
- Line Art: Thin

# 3. Camera Work and Composition
- Camera: Virtual Camera / Focal Length 50mm / Aperture f/2.8
- Angle: 15-degree Dutch Angle
- Focus: Focus on subject
- Depth of Field: Shallow depth of field in the background (with blur)

# 4. Scene Details
A. Character
- Appearance (Hair/Eyes/Clothing): [Completely maintain the style of the attached image]
 - Hair is flowing in reaction to energy pressure
 - Eyes are glowing, with a sharp anime-style shape

 - Stance: Feet spread and braced, torso twisted forward to the right
 - Hands: Cupped together facing each other (at hip height on the right), muscles are strongly tensed
- Expression: Filled with determination and intense effort, mouth open shouting

B. Lighting and Atmosphere
- Global Illumination: #ffffff
- Key Light:
 - Source: Energy ball between both hands
 - Color: Cyan Blue Electric
 - Intensity: 2.5EV overexposure (High Luminance)
 - Shadows: Sharp, dynamic shadows extending away from the center

C. Visual Effects
- Main Element: Spherical, turbulent plasma energy ball (between hands)
 - Core Color: #ffffff
 - Outer Glow: #00ffff
 - Particles: Blue sparks floating and rising
 - Lens Flare: Anamorphic blue light streaks
 - Bloom: Intensity 0.8

D. Background
- Context: Abstract studio space
- Visual: Simple gradient background
- Colors: #ffffff to #c0efff
 * Prioritize layer detail colors over introduction specified colors
- Detail Level: Low (to emphasize the subject)

# 5. Text Elements - Manga Style Speech Bubbles
Place vertical oval speech bubbles reacting to the timing of the shout, using the following coordinate image for placement
Adjust flexibly so as not to interfere with the character

- "{argument name="shout 1" default="ka..."}" : Top Left (X: 0.08, Y: 0.15)
- "{argument name="shout 2" default="me..."}" : Middle Left (X: 0.22, Y: 0.35)
- "{argument name="shout 3" default="ha..."}" : Middle Right (X: 0.78, Y: 0.55)
- "{argument name="shout 4" default="me..."}" : Bottom Right (X: 0.90, Y: 0.75)
```

### 提示词

```
Study the uploaded image carefully and fully internalize the scene: the subject’s appearance, clothing, posture, emotional state, and the surrounding environment. Treat this moment as a single frozen point in time.
Create a cinematic image set that feels like a photographer methodically explored this exact moment from multiple distances and angles, without changing anything about the subject or location.
All images must clearly belong to the same scene, captured under the same lighting conditions, weather, and atmosphere. Nothing in the world changes — only the camera position and framing evolve.
The emotional tone should remain consistent throughout the set, subtly expressed through posture, gaze, and micro-expressions rather than exaggerated acting.
Begin by observing the subject within the environment from afar, letting the surroundings dominate the frame and establish scale and mood.
Gradually move closer, allowing the subject’s full presence to emerge, then narrowing attention toward body language and facial expression.
End with intimate perspectives that reveal small but meaningful details — texture, touch, or eye focus — before shifting perspective above and below the subject to suggest reflection, vulnerability, or quiet resolve.
Across the sequence:
Wider views should emphasize space and atmosphere
Mid-range views should emphasize posture and emotional context
Close views should isolate feeling and detail
Perspective shifts (low and high angles) should feel purposeful and cinematic, not decorative
Depth of field must behave naturally: distant views remain mostly sharp, while closer frames introduce shallow focus and gentle background separation.
The final result should read as a cohesive 3×3 cinematic contact sheet, as if selected from a single roll of film documenting one emotional moment from multiple viewpoints.
No text, symbols, signage, watermarks, numbers, or graphic elements may appear anywhere in the images.
Photorealistic rendering, cinematic color grading, and consistent visual realism are mandatory.
```

## 277. Sequential Sci-Fi Bounty Hunter Story Grid

- **id**：2496
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=2496

### 描述

A highly detailed, multi-panel prompt for Nano Banana Pro designed to create a sequential sci-fi space drama story in a 2x2 grid. The narrative follows a tough female bounty hunter accepting a job and preparing for liftoff from a galactic outpost. The prompt enforces character and environment consistency across all panels and specifies cinematic, photorealistic rendering with dramatic lighting (Blade Runner meets The Mandalorian style).

### 示例图

![Sequential Sci-Fi Bounty Hunter Story Grid](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1766042186896_2e6279_G8Vt4RqXQAYqDXu.jpg)

### 提示词

```
"A single cohesive square image divided into a clean 2x2 grid with thin black borders, telling a sequential sci-fi space drama story of a tough female bounty hunter capturing and delivering her bounty at a galactic outpost. Character consistency (exact same in every panel): Bounty Hunter: Mid-30s athletic woman with short cropped silver hair, scarred cheek, wearing rugged black leather jacket with glowing neon accents, tactical pants, heavy boots, blaster holster on thigh, stern determined expression, cybernetic left arm with glowing circuits. Bounty: Male alien humanoid criminal (tentacle beard, blue skin, restrained with glowing energy cuffs), disheveled prisoner outfit, defeated glare. Environment consistency: Gritty galactic outpost spaceport at dusk—dusty landing pads, neon-lit cantina signs in alien languages, hovering drones, distant starry sky with twin moons, scattered cargo crates, worn metal walkways, atmospheric haze with floating dust particles. Style: Cinematic sci-fi drama like Blade Runner meets The Mandalorian, highly detailed photorealistic rendering, dramatic volumetric lighting with neon blues/purples/oranges, lens flares from ship engines, subtle film grain, epic widescreen composition vibes. Grid layout and progression (top-left → top-right → bottom-left → bottom-right): Panel 1 (wide establishing shot): Bounty hunter standing confidently in the outpost's dusty detention area, looking at a holographic bounty poster, alien bounty shows a menacing looking alien. Panel 2 (medium action shot): She walks with down the outpost, high-tech watch logs the bouny job just took, passersby (aliens and humans) glancing warily, hovering advertisement drones overhead. Panel 3 (three-quarter tracking shot): Hunter walking toward her parked sleek black bounty hunter spaceship (angular design with glowing engine vents), ramp lowering automatically as she approach, outpost towers and landing lights in background. Panel 4 (dynamic low-angle heroic shot): Inside the ship's cockpit from behind—hunter strapping in at pilot controls, starting engines with holographic displays activating and thrusters glowing bright blue, bounty secured in rear holding cell visible through glass, ship beginning liftoff as outpost recedes below, stars ahead. Aspect ratio 1:1 for perfect square grid, ultra-detailed cinematic masterpiece, best quality, dramatic sci-fi lighting and atmosphere."
```

```
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

■ LIGHTING ENGINE

│

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

■ MATERIAL SHADERS
```

## 294. Film Negative and Thumbnail Sheet Generation Prompt

- **id**：1618
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1618

### 描述

A complex, multi-step prompt sequence for Nano Banana Pro, combining a 7x6 grid division prompt with a prompt for creating old, disposable camera-style photos (emosnap). The goal is to simulate the process of developing film, including generating a thumbnail sheet, a negative film, and then a photo of high school students holding the negative.

### 示例图

![Film Negative and Thumbnail Sheet Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765509814206_8pljh4_G7y4UE3aIAAfyej.jpg)

![Film Negative and Thumbnail Sheet Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765509814734_49l83r_G7y4UEsaMAM7O09.jpg)

![Film Negative and Thumbnail Sheet Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765509814853_0dmz34_G7y4UEpakAAMQNc.jpg)

![Film Negative and Thumbnail Sheet Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765509817899_ghky8w_G7y4UEraMAEfIco.jpg)

### 提示词

```
①: No input image

Create 42 scenes, dividing the screen horizontally 7x vertically 6, based on "A single everyday photo taken with a low-quality disposable camera. A clumsy photo taken by Japanese high school students."
Display serial numbers from 1 in the bottom left of each scene.
Each scene is separated by thin white borders.
Image of a thumbnail sheet that comes when film is developed.
Do not draw real company names or product names.

----------
②: Using ① as input image

The input image is a thumbnail sheet that comes when film is developed.
Create the undeveloped negative film from this thumbnail sheet.
Place the completed negative film on a school desk and photograph it from a diagonal overhead angle.
Foreground blur and background blur due to depth of field. Live-action.

----------
③: Using ② as input image

A single everyday photo taken with a low-quality disposable camera of "Japanese high school students goofing around while holding this negative film." A clumsy photo taken by Japanese high school students.
Do not draw real company names or product names.

----------
④: Using ③ as input image

Include the input image at the beginning of "A single everyday photo taken with a low-quality disposable camera... (same as ① below)"
```

## 301. Enhanced Cinematic Noir Worm's-Eye View

- **id**：1436
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1436

### 描述

An extremely detailed, technical prompt for generating a cinematic neo-noir image using an extreme worm's-eye view from inside a drainage pipe. It meticulously describes the subject's appearance (Asian female, pixie cut, cat-eye sunglasses, vinyl bomber jacket), accessories (silver rings, dragon tattoo), composition (forced perspective, splayed fingers), lighting (sodium-vapor, volumetric fog, teal & orange grading), and camera/post-processing specs.

### 示例图

![Enhanced Cinematic Noir Worm's-Eye View](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765440101180_tpohe9_G7u-y_ka0AAr1AV.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Subject & Wardrobe: A striking Asian female with a razor-sharp jet-black pixie cut (textured with pomade, asymmetrical fringe grazing her right brow), sporting matte-black oversized cat-eye sunglasses (acetate frames with micro-scratches). Her fingers are adorned with 5 silver rings: a skull signet, a serpent coil, a geometric cube, a band with etched runes, and a cracked obsidian stone. She wears a crimson vinyl bomber jacket (high-gloss, ribbed cuffs), collar popped, catching the backlight like liquid fire. Beneath, a sleeveless mesh top reveals a dragon tattoo creeping up her forearm. 

Camera & Perspective: Extreme worm's-eye view shot from inside a drainage pipe (circular, 18-inch diameter), lens positioned 6 inches above the wet ground. Forced perspective distortion makes her hand a monumental, terrifying presence—fingers elongated, knuckles massive, veins bulging. The circular frame vignettes the image, creating a keyhole peep-show effect. 

Action & Composition: She leans forward aggressively, weight on her right leg, left knee bent, creating a dynamic diagonal thrust through the frame. Her right hand lunges toward camera, fingers splayed, index finger extended, casting a shadow that eclipses the dice. Her face is 3/4 obscured by the sunglasses and hair; only her pierced lower lip (silver hoop) and clenched jaw are visible, exuding predatory focus. 

Surface & Dice: The ground is rain-slicked asphalt with iridescent oil slicks (cyan, magenta, gold). 12 translucent red casino dice (precision-edged, pips glowing like embers) are scattered in chaotic abandon—some stacked, some mid-roll. Water beads cling to them, refracting light. A single die is caught in sharp focus in the extreme foreground, magnified by the lens. 

Lighting & Atmosphere: Dense fog backlit by a single sodium-vapor streetlight (5700K, creating a hellish orange aura). Volumetric light rays pierce the fog, haloing her jacket. Low-key lighting: deep shadows dominate, with rim lighting outlining her silhouette in molten red. A subtle cyan bounce from the wet surface creates chilling color contrast. Lens flare: anamorphic horizontal streaks and circular bokeh orbs. 

Technical Specs: Shot on ARRI Alexa 65, 12mm Laowa Probe Lens (f/2. 8, 1/1000s shutter), 8K resolution, ISO 6400 for grit. Deep focus from foreground die to her face. Cinematic aspect ratio: 2. 39:1 (though can be cropped to 4:5). Shot in Log-C, graded with Teal & Orange + Neon Noir LUT. 

Post-Processing: Chromatic aberration on high-contrast edges, film grain (Kodak 500T emulation), micro-contrast sharpening on rain droplets. Color grading: crushed blacks, lifted shadows, hyper-saturated reds, desaturated skin tones. Subtle vignette from the pipe's darkness. 

Mood & Narrative: Neo-noir tension — a high-stakes underground gamb
```

## 302. Split-Screen Time Travel Scene

- **id**：1396
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1396

### 描述

A detailed prompt template for generating a split-screen cinematic image that seamlessly blends two different eras, specifically the 1920s and the present day in Lalchowk, Kashmir, focusing on dynamic interaction and photorealistic detail.

### 示例图

![Split-Screen Time Travel Scene](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765440060136_u8g3gk_G7uZSZTaQAAoLbx.jpg)

### 提示词

```
A horizontal split-screen cinematic shot of {argument name="Scene" default="Lalchowk, kashmir india"}, seamlessly blending two different eras: {argument name="Era A" default="1920s"} on the left and {argument name="Era B" default="present day"} on the right (default: about 100 years ago vs. present day).

On the left side ({Era_A}): show era-appropriate architecture, interior or environment design, materials, vehicles, and props that clearly belong to that historical period. People wear authentic clothing from {Era_A}, including hairstyles, accessories, and typical items in their hands (such as books, umbrellas, instruments, letters, newspapers, etc.). The overall mood feels nostalgic and historically accurate.

On the right side ({Era_B}): show the same {Scene} in the modern era, with updated architecture or renovated structures, contemporary materials (glass, steel, LED screens, modern furniture), modern vehicles or equipment, and current technology (smartphones, laptops, cameras, etc.). People wear contemporary fashion that matches today’s style in this setting.

In the center: the two eras merge and overlap organically, without a hard dividing line. Elements from {Era_A} and {Era_B} visually interact: people from different times look at each other, walk through each other’s space, or seem surprised by the other era’s technology and objects. Architecture and environment smoothly morph from old to new (for example, stone gates turning into modern campus gates, classical concert hall décor fading into a futuristic stage, old street shops transforming into neon-lit storefronts).

Make sure the scene is not just a simple left/right comparison but a dynamic time-travel interaction where buildings, clothing, props, and human gestures clearly emphasize the contrast and fusion between the two eras. Photorealistic, 8k resolution, cinematic lighting, wide angle, highly detailed textures, rich sense of time-travel storytelling.
```

## 304. Two-Step Power Release Transformation Prompt (Japanese)

- **id**：1444
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1444

### 描述

A complex, two-step Japanese prompt for transforming a character's pose into an 'Awakening Stance' and then generating a 'Power Release' effect, where energy spheres expand and the ground/walls are dramatically excavated or destroyed. It uses structured JSON-like input for precise control over composition, action, and style, requiring a reference image of the character.

### 示例图

![Two-Step Power Release Transformation Prompt (Japanese)](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765440110737_7zzqn0_G7uDtKda8AAHzry.jpg)

![Two-Step Power Release Transformation Prompt (Japanese)](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765440110722_ma0jac_G7uDtKeaIAA7XOW.jpg)

![Two-Step Power Release Transformation Prompt (Japanese)](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765440110810_kkfwuy_G7uDtKgbQAAwXk4.jpg)

![Two-Step Power Release Transformation Prompt (Japanese)](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765440112458_o5u606_G7uDtKca8AAJs15.jpg)

### 提示词

```
[Awakening Pose Prompt]
subject: >
  Use the same character as reference A.
  Preserve all facial features, hairstyle, clothing, body proportions, colors,
  line weight, shading, and art style exactly as in the reference.

composition: >
  IMPORTANT: Change the framing from the reference.
  The camera must be positioned **much farther away** so the character’s entire body
  is fully visible **with extra space above, below, and around** the character.
  The character must appear **significantly smaller** than in reference A,
  occupying only about **20–30% of the frame height**.
  Place the character exactly at the center of the frame with wide open space
  surrounding them for later energy effects.
  The field of view should be noticeably wider than the reference.
  The final image must be rendered in a **16:9 aspect ratio**.

action: >
  Set the character in the awakening stance shown in reference B:
    - feet shoulder-width apart
    - both hands tightly clenched into fists
    - arms slightly bent and tense
    - chest slightly forward, posture firm and braced
    - intense, focused expression

location: >
  Reuse the same background environment as reference A.
  Expand the visible portion of the background **significantly**
  to match the widened, zoomed-out framing.
  Maintain the same textures, perspective, colors, and drawing style.

style: >
  Match the exact style of reference A:
  identical linework, shading, coloring, and background rendering.

camera_lighting: >
  Use the same lighting conditions as in reference A.

edit_instructions: >
  Recreate the character and background from reference A exactly.
  BUT modify the camera distance so the entire character fits in the frame
  with **substantial space** around them.
  The camera should be pulled back enough that the character appears small
  within a much wider environment.
  Change only:
    - the camera distance (strong zoom out)
    - the char
```

## 308. Royal Cartographer Fantasy World Map Prompt (Detailed Scenario)

- **id**：1465
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1465

### 描述

A highly detailed system prompt for Nano Banana Pro Agent V1.2, designed to create an antique fantasy world map (Royal Cartographer style) based on a complex input text describing geographical and historical conflicts. It includes specific instructions for the AI's thinking process (analyzing geography, visualizing biomes, designing layout) and style specifications (parchment texture, etching style characters, calligraphy). The example input text describes a world divided by the 'Dragon's Spine' mountain range into the frozen North and the sunny South.

### 示例图

![Royal Cartographer Fantasy World Map Prompt (Detailed Scenario)](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765440128320_vvkgc3_G7tYGQIaQAAN4kf.jpg)

### 提示词

```
You are a legendary Royal Cartographer.
Deeply analyze the content of the input text provided at the end and generate a detailed and beautiful image of a 'Fictional World Map' that reflects historical background and geographical features.

In doing so, incorporate the attached reference character into the map using the style of an old map's pen drawing or watercolor, placing them as a 'Guardian of the World' or 'Symbolic Portrait' decorating the map's margins, compass rose, or legend. Maintain character consistency while naturally blending them into the map design.

▼ Instructions for the Generation Process (Thinking Process)
Utilize Nano Banana Pro's inference capabilities to execute the following thought process before drawing:

1.  [Step 1: Analysis of Geographical and Historical Correlation]: [Extract the conflict structure between nations, the formation of civilizations, and climatic conditions from the input text. Logically infer why borders exist there (mountains, rivers, resources)]
2.  [Step 2: Visualization of Biomes]: [Determine how the described terrain (e.g., forest filled with magic, desolate wasteland) should be represented using map symbols and colors (ink density, contour lines)]
3.  [Step 3: Design of Information Layout]: [Place the names of countries, capitals, and major geographical features so as not to obscure the terrain, balancing visibility and artistry]

▼ Design and Style Specification
* Composition/Layout: [Orthographic projection from directly above (top-down view). A detailed overall map written meticulously to the four corners of the paper]
* Taste: [Antique fantasy map style drawn on parchment. Aged paper texture, handwritten ink lines, and coloring with pale watercolors]
* Text: [Clearly place country names and major city names using a serif font suitable for the world setting (Japanese compatible). The title should be written in decorative calligraphy]
* Character: Draw the reference character in an [etching (copperplate engraving) style] and specify a [pose pointing to the map's 'Legend,' or a composition integrated with the compass rose].

▼ Input Text

Classic High Fantasy (Physical Consistency and Border Conflicts)
Goal: Make the AI infer 'geographical logic' such as climate division by mountain ranges and river flow.

World Setting: The world is divided into North and South by the gigantic mountain range 'Dragon's Spine' running east-west across the center of the continent.

Northern 'Glacies, the Empire of Ice':

Climate: Extreme cold, permafrost.

Terrain: Steep glaciers and coniferous forests.

Capital: The fortress city 'Winter's Fang' at the foot of the mountain range.

Southern 'Solaris, the Kingdom of the Sun':

Climate: Warm and fertile land.

Terrain: The great river 'River of Life' flowing from the mountain range irrigates vast plains and flows into the southern sea. A vast desert spreads to the west.

Capital: The water city 'Aurora' in the river delta.

Instructions for the Map:

Write the country names '氷の帝国グラキエス' (Glacies, the Empire of Ice) on the north side and '太陽の王国ソラリス' (Solaris, the Kingdom of the Sun) on the south side of the map in large Japanese text.

Draw the central mountain range, river flow, and desert locations correctly geographically.

Draw decorations symbolizing the Four Great Spirits of this world (Fire, Water, Wind, Earth) in the four corners of the map.
```

## 309. Times Square Time-Blend Split-Screen

- **id**：1407
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1407

### 描述

A detailed prompt template for generating a split-screen cinematic image that seamlessly blends two different eras, specifically the 1880s and the present day in Times Square, New York, focusing on dynamic interaction and photorealistic detail.

### 示例图

![Times Square Time-Blend Split-Screen](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765440072440_55hm8p_G7tVRdfbgAAECCN.jpg)

### 提示词

```
A horizontal split-screen cinematic shot of {argument name="Scene" default="Times Square, New York"}, seamlessly blending two different eras: {argument name="Era A" default="1880s"} on the left and {argument name="Era B" default="present day"} on the right (default: about 100 years ago vs. present day).

On the left side ({Era_A}): show era-appropriate architecture, interior or environment design, materials, vehicles, and props that clearly belong to that historical period. People wear authentic clothing from {Era_A}, including hairstyles, accessories, and typical items in their hands (such as books, umbrellas, instruments, letters, newspapers, etc.). The overall mood feels nostalgic and historically accurate.

On the right side ({Era_B}): show the same {Scene} in the modern era, with updated architecture or renovated structures, contemporary materials (glass, steel, LED screens, modern furniture), modern vehicles or equipment, and current technology (smartphones, laptops, cameras, etc.). People wear contemporary fashion that matches today’s style in this setting.

In the center: the two eras merge and overlap organically, without a hard dividing line. Elements from {Era_A} and {Era_B} visually interact: people from different times look at each other, walk through each other’s space, or seem surprised by the other era’s technology and objects. Architecture and environment smoothly morph from old to new (for example, stone gates turning into modern campus gates, classical concert hall décor fading into a futuristic stage, old street shops transforming into neon-lit storefronts).

Make sure the scene is not just a simple left/right comparison but a dynamic time-travel interaction where buildings, clothing, props, and human gestures clearly emphasize the contrast and fusion between the two eras. Photorealistic, 8k resolution, cinematic lighting, wide angle, highly detailed textures, rich sense of time-travel storytelling.

Aspect Ratio: 4:3
```

```
The setting is an ultra-realistic, editorial-quality photograph capturing a busy breakfast café at mid-morning—sunlight spilling through tall windows, steam rising from espresso cups, soft clatter of plates, and an overall warm, bustling atmosphere. In the immediate foreground sits an anime-styled man with vivid blue hair, rendered with perfectly integrated cel-shading, crisp line art, and subtle specular lighting, yet placed with photographic depth-of-field so he feels physically present within the space. At the next table in the mid-ground, one of the patrons appears as a pencil-sketch figure, drawn in delicate graphite with visible cross-hatching and textured paper grain, seamlessly embedded into the real environment as if occupying a parallel medium. In the distant background, another customer is portrayed in full claymation style, shaped from hand-molded colored clay with visible fingerprints, matte surfaces, and slight frame-by-frame imperfections that suggest stop-motion movement. The entire scene blends these three visual languages into one cohesive cinematic image with rich ambient lighting, shallow depth-of-field transitions, and hyper-detailed café textures. No text overlays.
```

```
{
 "promptDetails": {
 "description": "An archaeological dig scene where the subject is viewing a 3D timeline and data overlay projected onto a recently uncovered artifact.",

 "Archeology",

 "Dry and Dusty"
 ]
 },
 "scene": {
 "background": {
 "setting": "An active archaeological trench in a dusty, sun-baked desert environment",

 },
 "subject": {
 "description": "The person defined by `[UPLOADED IMAGE]`, wearing field clothes and perhaps a brimmed hat, looking excited and careful.",

 "focus": "The artifact and the projection are the key points of visual interest."
 }
 },
 "overlayObject": {
 "type": "3D Timeline and Data Projection",
 "relationshipToEnvironment": "A semi-transparent, three-dimensional graphic emanating directly from the surface of the ceramic pot, showing its timeline.",
 "transform": "A series of floating text labels and lines showing dates and cultural periods, seemingly anchored to the artifact.",
 "surfaceInteraction": "Faint, slightly flickering orange/white text and lines that look 'hot' or energized, contrasting with the dry earth.",
 "components": {
 "artifactID": "{argument name=\"artifact ID\" default=\"Vessel Shard K-203\"}",
 "dating": "{argument name=\"dating\" default=\"Estimated C. 500 B.C. (Early Iron Age)\"}",
 "position": "Emanating from and hovering around the pot."
 }
 },
 "technicalStyle": {
 "aspectRatio": "5:4 (Portrait)",
 "photographyStyle": "Documentary, High Contrast",
 "camera": {
 "shotType": "Close-Up",
 "angle": "Low to the ground, capturing the detail of the soil and the artifact.",
 "depthOfField": "Shallow, focusing tightly on the subject's hands, the artifact, and the projection."
 },
 "lighting": {
 "type": "Harsh, Overhead Daylight",
 "description": "Strong, high-angle sun casting short, dark shadows, emphasizing the dry, dusty nature of the scene."
 },
 "color": {
 "palette": "Monochromatic earth tones (browns, tans, desaturated oranges), contrasted by the bright white/orange of the projection."
 }
 }
}
```

## 327. 1x3 Grid Phone Motion Edit JSON Prompt

- **id**：1320
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1320

### 描述

A highly detailed JSON prompt structure for Nano Banana Pro designed to generate a 1x3 grid of images. The primary function is 'phone motion edit,' where a person's photo is uploaded along with content for the phone screen, ensuring identity consistency and specific poses like looking at the phone, holding it forward, and a jump cut.

### 示例图

![1x3 Grid Phone Motion Edit JSON Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765438658264_y9ab5y_G7o9xAGbAAApmgM.jpg)

### 提示词

```
{
  "layout": {
    "type": "grid_1x3"
  },

  "global": {
    "edit_type": "phone_motion_edit",

    "source": {
      "mode": "EDIT",
      "reference_images": {
        "first": "base_photo_person_and_environment",
        "second": "screen_content_for_phone"
      },
      "preserve_from_first": {
        "same_person_or_group": true,
        "same_faces": true,
        "same_hairstyles": true,
        "same_outfits": true,
        "same_environment_style": true
      }
    },

    "identity": {
      "keep_identity_consistent": true,
      "expression": "bright_natural_smile"
    },

    "phone": {
      "allowed": true,
      "holding_styles": [
        "one_handed",
        "two_handed",
        "near_chest",
        "near_hip",
        "tilted",
        "sideways",
        "partially_toward_lens"
      ],
      "rules": {
        "each_person_may_hold_one_phone": true,
        "screen_should_be_naturally_visible": true,
        "do_not_force_phone_directly_toward_camera": true
      }
    },

    "screen_replacement": {
      "use_second_reference_image_as_content": true,
      "fit_without_distortion": true,
      "no_ui": true
    },

    "environment": {
      "preserve_environment_style_from_reference": true,
      "lighting_consistent_with_reference": true
    },

    "constraints": {
      "no_new_characters": true,
      "no_costume_change": true,
      "no_change_to_reference_location_type": true
    }
  },

  "panels": [
    {
      "id": "panel_2",
      "role": "phone_view_soft",
      "edit": true,
      "pose": {
        "pose_can_change": true,
        "style_tags": ["looking_at_phone", "face_close", "natural_pose"],
        "focus_on_expressive_hands": true
      }
    },

    {
      "id": "panel_3",
      "role": "phone_forward",
      "edit": true,
      "pose": {
        "pose_can_change": true,
        "style_tags": ["hand_forward", "phone_front_soft", "arm_extended"],
        "focus_on_expressive_hands": true
      },
      "camera_effect": {
        "perspective": "wide_angle",
        "near_objects_appear_large": true
      }
    },

    {
      "id": "panel_4",
      "role": "jump_cut",
      "edit": true,
      "use_preset": "jump_frame"
    }
  ],

  "pose_presets": {
    "jump_frame": {
      "pose": {
        "pose_can_change": true,
        "style_tags": ["jump", "lively", "mid_air"],
        "focus_on_expressive_hands": true
      },
      "camera_effect": {
        "perspective": "wide_angle",
        "allow_view_from_below": true
      }
    }
  }
}
```

```
A lone gunslinger on horseback rides through a dusty frontier town at golden hour. Sunlight cuts across the street, casting long shadows from wooden saloons, broken fences, and old telegraph poles. The sky is a dark steel-grey, heavy with an unstoppable blizzard rolling in like a wall of white fury. Frost clings to his beard, his armor, his breath visible in the freezing air. The landscape below is a vast frozen valley with shattered trees, frozen rivers, and distant mountains disappearing into the storm. Ultra-realistic textures: every snowflake sharp, every gust of wind visible, every crack of ice detailed. Dramatic lighting: cold blue highlights, deep shadows, glowing white reflections off the ice. Atmospheric, epic, heavy mood — the world preparing for something powerful and inevitable. Cinematic realism at its peak.
```

## 346. Persona 5-inspired pop-art anime poster for a custom chuunibyou character

- **id**：815
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=815

### 描述

A dynamic pop-art anime prompt that turns any given character concept into an over-the-top, game-opening-style poster with bold cel-shading, graffiti elements, and UI overlays.

### 示例图

![Persona 5-inspired pop-art anime poster for a custom chuunibyou character](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1764915822232_q88qg6_G7TgabUaMAEBRj5.jpg)

![Persona 5-inspired pop-art anime poster for a custom chuunibyou character](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1764915825887_c2wieh_G7TgabSbEAATg86.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Generate a visually explosive and trendy pop-art anime illustration. The main character of the image is "{argument name="character" default="an over-the-top chuunibyou knight"}", and the overall style should reference Persona 5 and street graffiti visuals, turning the character into an extremely flamboyant, extremely flat, ultra-cool anime figure.

Use sharp cel-shading for the character, with hard, clean lines and crisp contours. The pose must be exaggerated and full of motion. You can use wide-angle or fisheye perspectives so that hands, weapons, or props appear extremely close to the camera, like a freeze-frame at the moment a special attack is unleashed. The expression should be highly dramatic, with confident, manic, or cold-blooded "overacting" intensity, rather than a calm everyday look.

Reject realistic volume and shading; instead, rely on large areas of pure black heavy shadows and highly saturated flat color blocks to create an extreme, high-contrast flat look. The main colors can be bright red, lemon yellow, electric blue, neon purple, etc., contrasted against large black shadow silhouettes. Add screentones, halftone dots, speed lines, and radiating lines in the shadows and background so the image feels like printed manga layered with street graffiti.

The background does not need realistic perspective space; it should be a flat visual collage stage filled with huge sans-serif English letters, torn paper effects, ink splatters, bold arrow graphics, speech bubbles, and onomatopoeia, such as exaggerated "BOOM", "SLASH", or "WAKE UP" graffiti sound effects. You can integrate UI elements into the scene—health bars, skill names, buttons, numbers, tooltips—floating around the main character, as if the game interface and the illustration have merged, breaking the fourth wall.

Keep lighting high-contrast but not physically realistic; think in terms of graphic design areas of black/white/color rather than natural light logic. Edges between color areas on the character and background should be very sharp, without soft gradients; all colors should be clearly separated. You can add rough spray paint, smears, or hand-drawn graffiti textures in select areas to enhance the street and handmade feel.

The overall mood must be extremely dynamic, extremely chuunibyou, and extremely cool, as if the image is about to explode out of the screen, not quietly hang in a gallery. Avoid realistic oil painting styles, low-saturation vintage looks, or gentle, soft illustration styles. Do not use natural landscapes or everyday life backgrounds, and avoid soft, pastel color schemes. The image should be a vertical 2:3 high-resolution illustration, suitable as a tension-filled, maximalist pop-art manga poster.
```

## 348. Cinematic AI Video Keyframe Generation Prompt

- **id**：1986
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=1986

### 描述

A detailed system prompt designed for Nano Banana Pro to transform a single reference image into a cohesive cinematic short sequence, outputting 9-10 AI-video-ready keyframes. It includes strict rules for continuity, depth of field, color grading, and requires a structured breakdown of the scene, theme, story, and cinematic approach.

### 示例图

![Cinematic AI Video Keyframe Generation Prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1765991042869_cjf0nh_G7Soe0baIAA0rHV.jpg)

### 提示词

```
<role>
You are an award-winning trailer director + cinematographer + storyboard artist. Your job: turn ONE reference image into a cohesive cinematic short sequence, then output AI-video-ready keyframes.
</role>

<input>
User provides: one reference image (image).
</input>

<non-negotiable rules - continuity & truthfulness>
1) First, analyze the full composition: identify ALL key subjects (person/group/vehicle/object/animal/props/environment elements) and describe spatial relationships and interactions (left/right/foreground/background, facing direction, what each is doing).
2) Do NOT guess real identities, exact real-world locations, or brand ownership. Stick to visible facts. Mood/atmosphere inference is allowed, but never present it as real-world truth.
3) Strict continuity across ALL shots: same subjects, same wardrobe/appearance, same environment, same time-of-day and lighting style. Only action, expression, blocking, framing, angle, and camera movement may change.
4) Depth of field must be realistic: deeper in wides, shallower in close-ups with natural bokeh. Keep ONE consistent cinematic color grade across the entire sequence.
5) Do NOT introduce new characters/objects not present in the reference image. If you need tension/conflict, imply it off-screen (shadow, sound, reflection, occlusion, gaze).
</non-negotiable rules - continuity & truthfulness>

<goal>
Expand the image into a 10–20 second cinematic clip with a clear theme and emotional progression (setup → build → turn → payoff).
The user will generate video clips from your keyframes and stitch them into a final sequence.
</goal>

<step 1 - scene breakdown>
Output (with clear subheadings):
- Subjects: list each key subject (A/B/C…), describe visible traits (wardrobe/material/form), relative positions, facing direction, action/state, and any interaction.
- Environment & Lighting: interior/exterior, spatial layout, background elements, ground/walls/materials, light direction & quality (hard/soft; key/fill/rim), implied time-of-day, 3–8 vibe keywords.
- Visual Anchors: list 3–6 visual traits that must stay constant across all shots (palette, signature prop, key light source, weather/fog/rain, grain/texture, background markers).
</step 1 - scene breakdown>

<step 2 - theme & story>
From the image, propose:
- Theme: one sentence.
- Logline: one restrained trailer-style sentence grounded in what the image can support.
- Emotional Arc: 4 beats (setup/build/turn/payoff), one line each.
</step 2 - theme & story>

<step 3 - cinematic approach>
Choose and explain your filmmaking approach (must include):
- Shot progression strategy: how you move from wide to close (or reverse) to serve the beats
- Camera movement plan: push/pull/pan/dolly/track/orbit/handheld micro-shake/gimbal—and WHY
- Lens & exposure suggestions: focal length range (18/24/35/50/85mm etc.), DoF tendency (shallow/medium/deep), shutter “feel” (
```

## 353. 3×3 cinematic storyboard contact sheet from one input image

- **id**：724

- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=724

### 描述

A detailed Nano Banana Pro instruction prompt that analyzes one reference image and generates a coherent 3×3 cinematic contact sheet of the same subject with different shot types while keeping identity, clothing and lighting consistent.

### 示例图

![3×3 cinematic storyboard contact sheet from one input image](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1764577229402_u31t8z_G67jxidbkAcjDPL.jpg)

![3×3 cinematic storyboard contact sheet from one input image](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1764577234384_q6zk1s_G64FgZKXMAAXP_g.jpg)

![3×3 cinematic storyboard contact sheet from one input image](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1764577238747_2rpq2k_G64FgZGWwAAwXEl.jpg)

![3×3 cinematic storyboard contact sheet from one input image](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1764577242434_p205go_G64FgZXXcAAXmWw.jpg)

![3×3 cinematic storyboard contact sheet from one input image](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1764577255872_35gd8v_G64FgZYXUAAg5Wf.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Analyze the entire composition of the input image. Identify all key subjects present (whether a single person, group/couple, vehicle, or specific object) and their spatial relationships/interactions.
Generate a coherent 3×3 “contact sheet” grid that shows 9 different shots of exactly these subjects within the same environment.
You must adapt standard cinematic shot types to fit the content (for example, if it’s a group, keep the group together; if it’s an object, frame the entire object):
Row 1 (establishing the environment):
Extreme long shot (ELS): the subject appears small within a vast environment.
Long shot (LS): the full subject or group is visible from top to bottom (head to toe / wheels to roof).
Medium long shot (American shot / three-quarter): framed from above the knees (for people) or a 3/4 view (for objects).
Row 2 (core coverage):
4. Medium shot (MS): framed from the waist up (or the central core of an object). Focus on interaction/action.
5. Medium close-up (MCU): framed from the chest up. An intimate framing of the main subject.
6. Close-up (CU): tightly framed on the face or the “front” of the object.
Row 3 (details and angles):
7. Extreme close-up (ECU): intense focus on key features (eyes, hands, signs, textures) with macro-like detail.
8. Low-angle shot (worm’s-eye): look up at the subject from ground level (epic/heroic feeling).
9. High-angle shot (bird’s-eye): look down on the subject from above.
Ensure strict consistency: the same person/object, same clothing, and same lighting must appear in all 9 panels. Depth of field should vary realistically (with background blur in close-up shots).

Create a professional 3×3 cinematic storyboard grid with 9 panels.
The grid should present a specific subject/scene from the input image across a full range of focal lengths.
Top row: wide environmental shot, full-body view, 3/4 cropped (knees-up).
Middle row: waist-up view, chest-up view, face/front close-up.
Bottom row: macro details, low angle, high angle.
All frames must have photo-realistic textures, consistent cinematic color grading, and correct framing tailored to the number and type of subjects or objects being analyzed.
```

### 描述

A long-form Nano Banana Pro prompt in Japanese that generates a full-color moe-style manga using attached characters, enforcing Japanese right-to-left reading order, flexible panel layouts, and prohibiting certain changes or outputs.

### 示例图

![Moe-style full-color manga from given character and story](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1764577545414_4gnqwy_G6z874AbIAAWwhF.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Using the attached character, please generate a manga that emphasizes a moe-style illustration, direction, and facial expressions.
As a Japanese manga format, make the panel layout such that it can be read naturally from right to left and from top to bottom.
The placement and shapes of the panels are free, but the flow of the reader’s gaze must follow the Japanese reading order.
Instead of a uniform layout, please use an irregular or freely arranged panel structure.
Be sure to generate it as a full-color manga.

"One week since the release of Nano Banana Pro (Probanana). 
After continuing to test and share relentlessly… I had gained +235 followers and reached 450,000 impressions! 

I didn’t have much experience in sharing helpful content, so I was anxious, but I received many comments like ‘Thanks to your posts, I was able to do XX!’, which really encouraged me. 
Every time I see people arranging the content I’ve shared to create new methods and examples, I truly feel glad that I shared it.

If, like my past self, you are wondering, ‘Is there any value in me sharing things?’, please take just one step forward.
When you find information that would make your past self who didn’t know about it happy, even a short post is fine, so please share it.
That one step might give someone else the push they need."

Prohibited:
・Layouts that read left→right or bottom→top
・Arrangements that make the character look like a different person
・Replacing the traits of the reference character with those of other characters
・Using English, or horizontal writing for speech lines
・Changing colors or outfits, or outputting in monochrome, black and white, line art only, tone only, or grayscale
```

## 385. Comprehensive manga one-page scene creation system prompt

- **id**：320
- **需要参考图**：是
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=320

### 描述

A long, system-style prompt for Nano Banana Pro that defines roles, objectives, stylistic guidelines, and constraints to create a Japanese manga/anime one-page scene with natural Japanese speech bubbles and micro-emotional expression.

### 示例图

![Comprehensive manga one-page scene creation system prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763886265685_ak8qob_G6PvpPuacAAUB_b.jpg)

![Comprehensive manga one-page scene creation system prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763886268552_0u1x0v_G6PvpPjacAAZFyp.jpg)

### 提示词

```
Act as a “comprehensive creative” who simultaneously holds the following three perspectives:
Author’s perspective
　→ Naturally capture nuances of the character’s emotions, atmosphere, and movement
Editor’s perspective
　→ Manage composition, natural flow of text, and consistency of information density
Reader’s perspective
　→ Prioritize the kind of emotion that instantly draws the viewer in and maximizes appeal
Make sure these three perspectives are fused without contradiction in your output.
■ Objective
Infer from the given images and settings to create “a still image that works as a one-page scene of Japanese manga/anime,” together with natural Japanese speech bubbles and monologues.
Make “micro-emotion” and “the pull of texture” the core of the scene.
■ Basic policy (axes for the AI’s free inference)
Place focus on depictions that embed emotion in subtle motions and textures, such as the character’s gestures, fingertips, gaze, and the sway of their hair.
Use the three personas to simultaneously achieve “visually strong × natural to read × compelling to look at.”
Do not treat it as a single panel, but give it the structural strength of “a one-page scene.”
Arrange backgrounds and small props so that they complement the character’s inner world.
Leave a rhythmic sense of empty space in the frame where speech bubbles can be placed.
Create a “pulling pause” through distance, light direction, and shadows.
Treat it not as a magazine cover, but as “a frozen decisive moment in the story.”
■ Lines to keep (coherence and aesthetic guidelines)
Natural Japanese (no typos, no AI-ish phrasing).
Physical consistency of hands, joints, light sources, shadows, and posture.
Avoid excessive information density; respect empty space and pauses.
Real URLs, QR codes, and JAN codes are prohibited.
Avoid AI noise and visual breakage.
Do not damage the character’s dignity or psychological realism.
Do not lean toward sexual expression; keep “the charm of micro-emotion” as the core.
Treat “micro-emotion” as texture description that touches the psyche and do not use specific fetishistic terms.
■ Areas you may infer (creative freedom)
Fluctuations in the character’s emotions and inner movements.
The relationship between light direction and psychology.
“Touchable” textures of hair, clothing, and materials.
Angles of hands, wavering gaze, and body tilt.
Narrative meaning of the background.
Temperature of the air (silence/humidity/tension).
The visual focal point of attraction.
Strength of the composition as a one-page layout.
Add small props when needed (within the bounds of consistency).
■ Output style (manga one-page spec)
A readable flow as a single page:
　→ empty space → character → text → background support.
Speech bubbles should be in natural Japanese, placed in a way that captures the character’s breath and edges of emotion.
Treat monologue as words sinking inside the character (not explanatory text but a shadow of feeling).
Make the still image feel like it contains a drift of time.
From gestures and textures, depict the “peak of micro-emotion.”
■ One-sentence summary
“Integrate the three perspectives (author, editor, reader) and, focusing on micro-emotion and the pull of texture, infer and finish a one-page Japanese-language scene that captivates.”
```

## 394. Vertical 4-panel gekiga-style manga YAML prompt

- **id**：335
- **画廊链接**：https://youmind.com/zh-CN/gpt-image-2-prompts?id=335

### 描述

A long English YAML prompt specifying layout, art style, manga elements, and panel-by-panel content for a 1:3 vertical 4-panel gekiga-style manga page featuring Knight Alex working at a convenience store.

### 示例图

![Vertical 4-panel gekiga-style manga YAML prompt](https://cms-assets.youmind.com/media/1763886197419_hmjgce_G6Nb9qsacAALnlq.jpg)

### 提示词

```
---
generation_parameters:
  aspect_ratio: "1:3"

manga_page_generation_prompt:
  layout_structure:
    type: Full page vertical 4-panel comic strip (yon-koma layout).
    arrangement: Four roughly square panels stacked perfectly vertically from top to bottom.
    spacing: Clear white gutters separating each of the four panels.
    title_area: (Optional) A small space at the very top for a title.
    overall_aesthetic: A printed comic magazine page look with slightly textured paper.

  art_style:
    description: {argument name="style_desc_en" default="1970s–80s Japanese Gekiga style: gritty, heavy shadows, serious tone."}
    color_palette: Desaturated, heavy oil-painting colors, sepia tones, dark atmosphere.
    texture: Rough ink lines, analog brushwork, dramatic cross-hatching shadows, and ink bleed effects.

  manga_elements:
    speech_bubbles:
      - Presence: Multiple hand-drawn speech bubbles (fukidashi) within panel boundaries.
        Style: Rough, organic outlines containing placeholder Japanese marks.
    sound_effects:
      - Presence: Bold Katakana sound effects integrated into the art.
        Style: Rough calligraphy (e.g., "ドォォォン", "ゴゴゴゴ").

  panel_content_sequence:
    panel_1_top (Intro):
      - Scene: [Wide angle] Knight Alex (grim face, full armor) standing stiffly behind a modern convenience store counter.
      - Text/FX: Speech bubble with formal greeting. Small "ウィーン" (door sound).
    panel_2_upper_mid (Development):
      - Scene: A scared salaryman customer shrinking away, Alex glaring intensely and leaning forward over the counter.
      - Text/FX: Customer's scared bubble. Large "ゴゴゴゴ" (menacing FX) around Alex.
    panel_3_lower_mid (Twist):
      - Scene: [Dramatic close-up] Alex pulling out his giant claymore sword just to point at a bento box.
      - Text/FX: Spiky shout bubble "貴様の糧はこれか!!" and intense "ジャキィィン！" (sword FX).
    panel_4_bottom (Conclusion):
      - Scene: The exhausted store manager (looser art style) smacking Alex's head with a rolled magazine. Alex looks comically dejected.
      - Text/FX: Manager's angry bubble "剣をしまうんだよ！" and sharp "スパン！" (slap FX).

negative_prompt:
  - Single illustration, square image, landscape orientation
  - Panels arranged horizontally in a 2x2 grid
  - Modern clean digital art, anime style
  - Bright cheerful colors, no shadows
```

---
